# Codex conversation 01a0a5e8-ff60-7b41-88e0-0879738408bf

## assistant

<proposed_plan>
# LSAT U booking confirmation upgrade

## Summary

Show confirmation immediately after payment, then update it automatically when Google finishes creating and sending the invitation. Replace the sparse page with a Calendly-inspired LSAT U confirmation card, including the requested corner ribbon and a calendar-only action.

## Implementation changes

- Start from the latest remote `main`, preserving the existing dirty local branch and Lovable’s recent changes.
- Expand the protected booking-status response with:
  - Attendee name
  - Session title
  - Start and end time
  - Duration
  - Booking timezone
  - Calendar readiness
  - Google Calendar event URL
- Persist the timezone selected during booking. Existing bookings fall back to the browser timezone, then `America/Toronto`.
- Show the final confirmation page immediately:
  - Initially: “You are scheduled!” and “We’re preparing your calendar invitation.”
  - Continue polling while Google Calendar finishes.
  - Once ready: “A calendar invitation has been sent to your email address.”
  - Enable one primary “Open Invitation” button linked to the verified Google Calendar event.
  - If preparation is delayed, keep the booking confirmed and show a truthful recovery message instead of spinning indefinitely.
- Build the meeting-details card with the session title, attendee name, localized date and time, duration, timezone, and a note that Google Meet details are included in the calendar invitation.
- Add a diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” corner ribbon. Do not reproduce Calendly trademarks, assets, menu, or copy-link controls.
- Keep the confirmation page calendar-only, with no separate Meet button.
- Correct the email wording to past tense: the invitation “has been sent,” not “is on its way.” Retain the useful Meet action in the email.
- Replace the hardcoded 70-minute email duration and Toronto timezone with values from the booking.
- Update the outbox worker to perform a bounded second claim after calendar creation, allowing newly unblocked confirmation emails to send during the same webhook-triggered run instead of waiting up to one minute.
- Preserve the required ordering: payment finalized, calendar event and Meet created, Google invitation sent, confirmation email sent. Do not claim that Google has placed the event directly onto every recipient’s calendar because recipient settings can still require acceptance.

## Public interface changes

- Extend the capability-protected status payload with `attendee_name`, `event_label`, `duration_minutes`, `timezone`, `calendar_ready`, and `calendar_url`.
- Add a validated IANA timezone to the booking hold/finalization data.
- Resolve `calendar_url` from the mirrored Google event only after validating the booking capability token.

## Test and acceptance plan

- Test immediate booked rendering followed by automatic calendar-ready updating.
- Test calendar button enablement, delayed-calendar recovery, expired tokens, and unauthorized status requests.
- Test dynamic session titles, durations, timezones, and daylight-saving formatting.
- Test that Google promotion completes before email eligibility and that the same worker invocation drains newly unblocked email jobs without duplicates.
- Render-test the email to confirm the corrected wording and accurate booking details.
- Browser-test narrow, medium, wide, and side-panel layouts, including ribbon placement, keyboard access, focus states, and screen-reader labels.
- After deployment, run one separately authorized $1 live booking and verify the Stripe payment, confirmation page, Google invitation, Meet details, and confirmation email end to end.

## Assumptions

- The requested “automatic” behavior means LSAT U sends the invitation immediately. Google still controls whether an external invitation appears automatically or awaits acceptance.
- Deploying the Edge Function through Lovable requires Joshua’s explicit confirmation immediately before any action that may spend Lovable credits.
- No real payment or Lovable credit will be used during implementation without that fresh confirmation.
</proposed_plan>

<proposed_plan>
# Match the Calendly confirmation experience with LSAT U branding

## Summary

Use the supplied screenshot as the visual source of truth for both the confirmation email and the booking-success page. Preserve its restrained layout and information hierarchy, replacing every Calendly element with LSAT U branding and real booking data.

## Confirmation email

- Send from `LSAT U <noreply@lsatprep.study>`.
- Replace the current decorative gold confirmation card with the screenshot’s clean, white, centered email layout.
- Reproduce the visible structure:
  - Centered LSAT U logo
  - Light dotted divider
  - Left-aligned content column
  - `Hi [first name],`
  - `Your LSAT U session has been scheduled.`
  - Bold field labels with values beneath them
  - Event type
  - Invitee name
  - Invitee email
  - Date and time
  - Duration
  - Timezone
  - Location: Google Meet
- Use LSAT U’s existing typography and colors while matching the screenshot’s generous whitespace, narrow content width, light dividers, and minimal styling.
- Keep a Google Meet button lower in the email, followed by plain fallback links for clients that block styled buttons.
- Change the inaccurate sentence to: “Your Google Calendar invitation has been sent.”
- Remove “is on its way” and do not imply that the LSAT U confirmation email is waiting for calendar creation.
- Populate duration and timezone from the booking instead of hardcoding 70 minutes and Toronto.
- Build with email-safe tables and inline styles so Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, and mobile clients remain visually consistent.

## Gmail event panel

- Continue creating the real Google Calendar event with the invitee as an attendee, Google Meet attached, and `sendUpdates: "all"`.
- Add validated `EventReservation` structured metadata to the LSAT U confirmation email with the event title, attendee, start time, location, reservation number, and secure reservation URL.
- Add a Gmail `ViewAction` targeting the booking’s protected reservation page.
- Validate the markup with Google’s Email Markup Tester.
- Prepare the required Google sender-registration submission using the production sender, matching SPF or DKIM, `From`, and return-path domains. Google requires registration before marked-up production emails reliably receive enhanced Gmail treatment. [Google registration requirements](https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/registering-with-google)
- Treat Gmail’s blue event panel as an enhancement, not part of the HTML email. Gmail controls its exact appearance, labels, conflict warnings, and available buttons. LSAT U can provide the correct event metadata but cannot force Gmail to render an identical panel. [EventReservation specification](https://developers.google.com/workspace/gmail/markup/reference/types/EventReservation)

## Confirmation page

- Show “You are scheduled!” immediately after payment confirmation.
- Mirror the earlier Calendly confirmation-page structure:
  - Sent-calendar-invitation message
  - Calendar-only primary action
  - Session-details panel
  - Attendee name
  - Localized date and time
  - Duration
  - Timezone
  - Google Meet availability note
  - Diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” corner ribbon
- While Google is still creating the event, show “Preparing your calendar invitation” and keep the calendar action disabled.
- Continue polling after the booking becomes `booked`.
- Replace the preparing state automatically with “A calendar invitation has been sent to your email address” and enable “Open Invitation” when the Google event URL becomes available.
- If Google is delayed, keep the booking confirmed and display a recovery message instead of spinning indefinitely.

## Backend and interface changes

- Persist the timezone selected during booking.
- Extend the capability-protected status response with `attendee_name`, `event_label`, `duration_minutes`, `timezone`, `calendar_ready`, and `calendar_url`.
- Resolve `calendar_url` from the mirrored Google event only after validating the booking token.
- Make the outbox worker claim newly unblocked confirmation-email jobs again after calendar promotion, so the email can send in the same webhook-triggered run.
- Preserve idempotency and the required ordering: payment finalized, calendar and Meet created, Google invitation sent, confirmation email sent.

## Verification

- Snapshot-test the email at desktop and mobile widths against the supplied reference.
- Render-test Gmail, Outlook, and Apple Mail output.
- Test real booking values, long names, different durations, multiple timezones, daylight-saving transitions, and missing optional notes.
- Test the immediate confirmation page, calendar-preparing transition, successful calendar link, delayed-calendar recovery, and invalid booking tokens.
- Verify the structured metadata with Google’s official validator.
- After deployment, perform one separately authorized $1 production booking and verify Stripe payment, calendar invitation, Meet link, Gmail presentation, confirmation page, and LSAT U email.
- Lovable deployment or any action that may consume Lovable credits requires Joshua’s fresh confirmation immediately before execution.

## Assumptions

- “Exactly” means faithfully reproducing the reference email’s layout and hierarchy with LSAT U branding and booking-specific content.
- Gmail’s surrounding interface and generated blue event panel cannot be pixel-controlled by the email template.
- Google may still require the recipient to accept an external invitation depending on that recipient’s Calendar settings.
</proposed_plan>

# LSAT U burgundy booking confirmation

## Summary

Match the supplied Calendly reference using LSAT U branding and its existing burgundy palette. The email body and confirmation page will use burgundy instead of blue. Gmail’s generated event panel may remain blue because Google controls that interface.

## Visual specification

- Use LSAT U’s production booking color:
  - Primary: `hsl(347 70% 27%)`, approximately `#75152A`
  - Dark gradient: `#4F0D1C → #6E1423 → #8E1D3A`
  - White button and ribbon text
- Use exact inline hex colors in email HTML because email clients cannot access the website’s CSS variables.
- Preserve the reference’s white background, generous spacing, narrow content column, understated dividers, and simple typography.
- Replace the blue Calendly logo with the LSAT U mark in burgundy.
- Use burgundy for buttons, links, important icons, and the “Powered by LSAT U” ribbon.
- Do not make the entire email burgundy or reproduce any Calendly branding.

## Confirmation email

- Recreate the reference structure:
  - Centered burgundy LSAT U logo
  - Light divider
  - `Hi [first name],`
  - `Your LSAT U session has been scheduled.`
  - Event type
  - Invitee name and email
  - Date, time, duration, and timezone
  - Google Meet location
- Change the outdated message to: “Your Google Calendar invitation has been sent.”
- Keep a burgundy Google Meet button and plain fallback link.
- Use booking-derived duration and timezone instead of hardcoded values.
- Build with email-safe tables and inline styles for Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, and mobile clients.

## Gmail event panel

- Continue sending the actual Google Calendar invitation automatically with the attendee and Meet link.
- Add validated `EventReservation` metadata and a secure reservation action.
- Register the production sender with Google if LSAT U qualifies for enhanced Gmail markup.
- Accept that Gmail controls the generated panel’s blue background, button labels, conflict warnings, and final appearance. LSAT U cannot recolor Gmail’s own interface.

## Confirmation page

- Show “You are scheduled!” immediately after payment.
- Use the same burgundy palette for the success icon, calendar button, links, borders, and diagonal “Powered by LSAT U” ribbon.
- Display attendee, session type, date, time, duration, timezone, and conferencing information.
- Provide one calendar-only “Open Invitation” action.
- Continue polling while the calendar invitation is prepared, then enable the button automatically.
- Show a recoverable confirmed state if Google Calendar is delayed.

## Backend and verification

- Persist the selected timezone.
- Extend the protected booking-status response with the display details, calendar readiness, and calendar URL.
- Allow the outbox worker to send newly unblocked confirmation emails during the same run after calendar creation.
- Preserve payment, calendar, invitation, and email idempotency.
- Compare desktop and mobile email renders against the supplied screenshot.
- Test Gmail, Outlook, Apple Mail, different durations and timezones, delayed calendar creation, and invalid booking tokens.
- Perform one separately authorized $1 production booking after deployment.
- Obtain fresh confirmation before any Lovable action that could consume credits.

## Assumptions

- “Instead of blue” applies to all LSAT U-controlled email and confirmation-page elements.
- Gmail’s surrounding interface and generated event panel are excluded because their colors cannot be controlled by LSAT U.
</proposed_plan>

Use the absolute paths above.\n\n## Project Map\n\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\n\n## Stack And Commands\n\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\n- Build: `npm run build`\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\n- Test: `npm run test`\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\n\n## Working Rules\n\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\n\n## Product Constraints\n\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\n\n## Smart Drill Note\n\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\n\n## Verification (must not skip)\n\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed "fix" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\n\n## Workflow Expectations\n\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\n\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\n\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\n\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\n\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong "Done" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\n\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\n\n## Codex Adapter Layout\n\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\n\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\n\n## Known Gaps\n\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\n\n## Agent skills\n\n### Issue tracker\n\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n\n### Triage labels\n\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\n\n### Domain docs\n\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\n\r\n'}]

These two separate cases share some relationship, so they are analogous in at least one respect.\\n\\nAnalogies show up a lot on **Method** of reasoning questions, which ask us to characterize the type of evidence or type of move the author used.\\n\\nWhen we see a **fill-in-the-blank** question (aka Logical Completion), the paragraph is leading us to some final idea, and they frequently use a **Complete the Analogy** type structure, as I did at the top of this page.\\n\\nWhen we see analogies show up in the **Assumption Family**, they tend to go like this: \\"Because X and Y are similar when it comes to Thing A, they\'re probably also similar when it comes to Thing B\\".\\n\\n_Five years ago, my family went to Lake Crocus and I didn\'t have any fun. end_at: string };\nexport type SingleSessionStatus = {\n  status: "draft" | "checkout_open" | "booked" | "cancelled" | "expired" | "abandoned" | "refund_pending" | "refunded" | "refund_failed";\n  start_at: string;\n  end_at: string;\n  meet_url?: string | null;\n  checkout_url?: string | null;\n};\ntype HoldInput = {\n  start_at: string;\n  name: string;\n  email: string;\n  notes: string;\n  guests: string[];\n  timezone: string;\n  client_idempotency_key: string;\n  turnstile_token: string;\n  booking_token?: string;\n};\n// A free event finishes inside `hold`: there is no checkout to send anyone to,\n// so the url and session id come back null and the booking is already booked.\ntype HoldResult = {\n  checkout_url: string | null;\n  booking_token: string;\n  checkout_session_id: string | null;\n  booking?: SingleSessionStatus;\n};\n\nexport type EventInfo = {\n  host: { name: string; title: string };\n  duration_minutes: number;\n  amount_cents: number;\n  currency: string;\n  requires_payment?: boolean;\n  /** True for a one-off link: this URL sells one session and then stops. */\n  single_use?: boolean;\n  /** False once a one-off link has been used. Undefined for ordinary events. */\n  available?: boolean;\n};\n\n/** "1h 10m", "30m" -- the event\'s own length, not a constant. */\nfunction durationLabel(minutes: number): string {\n  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n  const rest = minutes % 60;\n  if (!hours) return `${rest}m`;\n  return rest ? `${hours}h ${rest}m` : `${hours}h`;\n}\n\n/** The wire words the edge function uses, as something a person can read. */\nconst ERROR_TEXT: Record<string, string> = {\n  unknown_event: "This booking link is no longer active.",\n  link_already_used: "This link has already been used to book a session.",\n  slot_unavailable: "That time was taken while you were filling this in. Please pick another.",\n  rate_limited: "Too many attempts. Please wait a moment and try again.",\n  security_check_failed: "The security check did not pass. Please reload and try again.",\n  security_check_unavailable: "The security check could not load. Please reload and try again.",\n  security_check_expired: "The security check expired. Please try again.",\n  verification_failed: "The security check did not pass. Please reload and try again.",\n};\n\nfunction readableError(message: string): string {\n  return ERROR_TEXT[message] ?? message;\n}\n\nexport type SingleSessionApi = {\n  info: () => Promise<EventInfo>;\n  slots: () => Promise<{ slots: SingleSessionSlot[] }>;\n  hold: (input: HoldInput) => Promise<HoldResult>;\n  status: (checkoutSessionId: string, bookingToken: string) => Promise<SingleSessionStatus>;\n  abandon: (checkoutSessionId: string, bookingToken: string) => Promise<{ status: string }>;\n  cancel: (cancelToken: string, confirm: boolean) => Promise<SingleSessionStatus>;\n  getTurnstileToken: () => Promise<string>;\n  redirectToCheckout: (url: string) => void;\n};\n\ntype PendingCheckout = HoldResult;\nconst PENDING_KEY = "lsatu.single-session.pending";\nconst IDEMPOTENCY_KEY = "lsatu.single-session.idempotency";\n// The single session keeps the original, unsuffixed keys so a hold already in\n// someone\'s browser survives this change. Other events get their own namespace\n// so a held consultation and a held session can never read each other\'s token.\nconst pendingKey = (event: string) =>\n  event === "single-session" ? PENDING_KEY : `${PENDING_KEY}.${event}`;\nconst idempotencyKeyFor = (event: string) =>\n  event === "single-session" ? IDEMPOTENCY_KEY : `${IDEMPOTENCY_KEY}.${event}`;\n\ndeclare global {\n  interface Window {\n    turnstile?: {\n      render: (element: HTMLElement, options: Record<string, unknown>) => string;\n      remove: (widgetId: string) => void;\n    };\n  }\n}\n\nasync function invoke<T>(action: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<T> {\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("single-session", { body: { action, ...extra } });\n  if (error) {\n    // supabase-js turns any non-2xx into "Edge Function returned a non-2xx\n    // status code" and keeps the body on the error object. That body is where\n    // the reason lives -- unknown_event, link_already_used -- and it is the\n    // whole difference between "something went wrong" and "this link is spent".\n    const body = (error as { context?: { json?: () => Promise<{ error?: string }> } }).context;\n    const reason = await body?.json?.().then((parsed) => parsed?.error).catch(() => undefined);\n    throw new Error(reason || error.message);\n  }\n  const result = data as { error?: string } & T;\n  if (result?.error) throw new Error(result.error);\n  return result;\n}\n\nfunction turnstileToken(): Promise<string> {\n  const sitekey = import.meta.env.VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY as string | undefined;\n  if (!sitekey) return Promise.reject(new Error("security_check_unavailable"));\n  const run = () => new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {\n    if (!window.turnstile) return reject(new Error("security_check_unavailable"));\n    const mount = document.createElement("div");\n    mount.hidden = true;\n    document.body.appendChild(mount);\n    let widgetId = "";\n    const clean = () => {\n      if (widgetId) window.turnstile?.remove(widgetId);\n      mount.remove();\n    };\n    widgetId = window.turnstile.render(mount, {\n      sitekey,\n      size: "invisible",\n      callback: (token: string) => { clean(); resolve(token); },\n      "error-callback": () => { clean(); reject(new Error("security_check_failed")); },\n      "expired-callback": () => { clean(); reject(new Error("security_check_expired")); },\n    });\n  });\n  if (window.turnstile) return run();\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const existing = document.querySelector<HTMLScriptElement>(\'script[data-lsatu-turnstile="true"]\');\n    const script = existing ?? document.createElement("script");\n    if (!existing) {\n      script.src = "https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit";\n      script.async = true;\n      script.defer = true;\n      script.dataset.lsatuTurnstile = "true";\n      document.head.appendChild(script);\n    }\n    script.addEventListener("load", () => void run().then(resolve, reject), { once: true });\n    script.addEventListener("error", () => reject(new Error("security_check_unavailable")), { once: true });\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * One api per event. Every call names the event it is about, so the same page\n * serves the paid session and the free consultation without either of them\n * being able to answer for the other.\n */\nexport function makeApi(eventSlug: string): SingleSessionApi {\n  return {\n    info: () => invoke("info", { event: eventSlug }),\n    slots: () => invoke("slots", { event: eventSlug }),\n    hold: (input) => invoke("hold", { ...input, event: eventSlug }),\n    status: (checkoutSessionId, bookingToken) => invoke("status", { event: eventSlug, checkout_session_id: checkoutSessionId, booking_token: bookingToken }),\n    abandon: (checkoutSessionId, bookingToken) => invoke("abandon", { event: eventSlug, checkout_session_id: checkoutSessionId, booking_token: bookingToken }),\n    cancel: (cancelToken, confirm) => invoke("cancel", { event: eventSlug, cancel_token: cancelToken, confirm }),\n    getTurnstileToken: turnstileToken,\n    redirectToCheckout: (url) => window.location.assign(url),\n  };\n}\n\nconst defaultApi: SingleSessionApi = makeApi("single-session");\n\nfunction readPending(event = "single-session"): PendingCheckout | null {\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(pendingKey(event)) ?? "null") as Partial<PendingCheckout> | null;\n    return parsed?.booking_token && parsed.checkout_session_id && parsed.checkout_url ? parsed as PendingCheckout : null;\n  } catch { return null; }\n}\n\nfunction viewerTimezone() {\n  try { return Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || "America/Toronto"; }\n  catch { return "America/Toronto"; }\n}\n\nfunction formatTime(iso: string, hour12: boolean, timeZone?: string) {\n  return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit", hour12, ...(timeZone ? { timeZone } : {}) });\n}\n\nfunction formatSlot(iso: string) {\n  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString(undefined, { weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric", year: "numeric", hour: "numeric", minute: "2-digit" });\n}\n\nfunction statusCopy(status: SingleSessionStatus["status"]) {\n  if (status === "cancelled") return { title: "Your session is cancelled", body: "The calendar invitation is being removed. Cancelling does not automatically issue a refund." };\n  if (status === "refund_pending") return { title: "We\'re returning your payment", body: "That time was no longer available when payment finished. Your refund is being processed, and we\'ll email you when Stripe confirms it." };\n  if (status === "refunded") return { title: "Your payment was refunded", body: "That time could not be booked. Stripe has confirmed the refund." };\n  if (status === "refund_failed") return { title: "Your refund needs attention", body: "Joshua has been alerted and will follow up with you directly." };\n  if (status === "abandoned") return { title: "This hold was released", body: "No booking was made. You can choose another available time." };\n  return { title: "This hold expired", body: "No booking was made. Choose another available time to start again." };\n}\n\nfunction ResultView({ state, checkoutSessionId }: { state: SingleSessionStatus; checkoutSessionId?: string | null }) {\n  if (state.status === "booked") {\n    return <main className="booking-holo min-h-screen px-4 py-12 sm:py-20">\n      <section className="booking-shadow mx-auto max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center sm:p-10" aria-live="polite">\n        <div className="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-primary"><Check className="h-6 w-6" /></div>\n        <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">You\'re booked</h1>\n        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{formatSlot(state.start_at)}</p>\n        {state.meet_url ? <Button asChild className="mt-6"><a href={state.meet_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer"><Video className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />Join Google Meet</a></Button>\n          : <p className="mt-6 rounded-xl bg-muted px-4 py-3 text-sm">Your calendar invite is on its way. It will include the Google Meet link.</p>}\n      </section>\n    </main>;\n  }\n  if (state.status === "checkout_open" || state.status === "draft") {\n    return <main className="booking-holo min-h-screen px-4 py-12 sm:py-20">\n      <section className="booking-shadow mx-auto max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center sm:p-10" aria-live="polite">\n        <Loader2 className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-primary motion-reduce:animate-none" />\n        <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-semibold">Payment received, finalizing</h1>\n        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">You\'ll have an email shortly. If you contact support, include checkout session {checkoutSessionId ?? "unknown"}.</p>\n      </section>\n    </main>;\n  }\n  const copy = statusCopy(state.status);\n  return <main className="booking-holo min-h-screen px-4 py-12 sm:py-20">\n    <section className="booking-shadow mx-auto max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center sm:p-10" role="status">\n      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{copy.title}</h1><p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{copy.body}</p>\n      <Button asChild className="mt-6"><Link to="/book/session">See available times</Link></Button>\n    </section>\n  </main>;\n}\n\nexport default function SingleSession({\n  api,\n  event = "single-session",\n}: { api?: SingleSessionApi; event?: string }) {\n  // Built once per event. A caller that hands in its own api (the tests) still wins.\n  const resolvedApi = useMemo(() => api ?? (event === "single-session" ? defaultApi : makeApi(event)), [api, event]);\n  const [params] = useSearchParams();\n  const bookingToken = params.get("booking_token");\n  const checkoutSessionId = params.get("checkout_session_id");\n  const cancelToken = params.get("cancel_token");\n  const [info, setInfo] = useState<EventInfo | null>(null);\n  const [slots, setSlots] = useState<SingleSessionSlot[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [pickedDay, setPickedDay] = useState<Date | null>(null);\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<SingleSessionSlot | null>(null);\n  const [monthAnchor, setMonthAnchor] = useState(() => startOfDay(new Date()));\n  const [hour12, setHour12] = useState(true);\n  const [name, setName] = useState("");\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");\n  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");\n  const [guests, setGuests] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [result, setResult] = useState<SingleSessionStatus | null>(null);\n  const [resume, setResume] = useState<PendingCheckout | null>(null);\n  const [cancelPreview, setCancelPreview] = useState<SingleSessionStatus | null>(null);\n  const submitPromise = useRef<Promise<void> | null>(null);\n  const availabilityHeading = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);\n  const [timezone, setTimezone] = useState(viewerTimezone);\n  const timezoneOptions = useMemo(() => {\n    try {\n      const values = (Intl as typeof Intl & { supportedValuesOf?: (key: "timeZone") => string[] }).supportedValuesOf?.("timeZone") ?? [];\n      return values.includes(timezone) ? values : [timezone, ...values];\n    } catch { return [timezone]; }\n  }, [timezone]);\n\n  const loadAvailability = useCallback(async (rejectedStartAt?: string) => {\n    const [nextInfo, nextSlots] = await Promise.all([resolvedApi.info(), resolvedApi.slots()]);\n    const availableSlots = (nextSlots.slots ?? []).filter((slot) => slot.start_at !== rejectedStartAt);\n    setInfo(nextInfo);\n    setSlots(availableSlots);\n    const first = availableSlots[0];\n    if (first) {\n      const date = startOfDay(new Date(first.start_at));\n      setPickedDay((current) => current ?? date);\n      setMonthAnchor(new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), 1));\n    }\n  }, [resolvedApi]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    async function start() {\n      try {\n        if (cancelToken) {\n          const preview = await resolvedApi.cancel(cancelToken, false);\n          if (!cancelled) setCancelPreview(preview);\n          return;\n        }\n        const pending = readPending(event);\n        const token = bookingToken ?? pending?.booking_token;\n        const session = checkoutSessionId ?? pending?.checkout_session_id;\n        if (token && session) {\n          const state = await resolvedApi.status(session, token);\n          if (cancelled) return;\n          if (!checkoutSessionId && state.status === "checkout_open" && pending) setResume(pending);\n          else setResult(state);\n          return;\n        }\n        await loadAvailability();\n      } catch (e) {\n        if (!cancelled) setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "booking_unavailable");\n      } finally { if (!cancelled) setLoading(false); }\n    }\n    void start();\n    return () => { cancelled = true; };\n  }, [api, bookingToken, cancelToken, checkoutSessionId, loadAvailability]);\n\n  const resultStatus = result?.status;\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!resultStatus || !checkoutSessionId || !bookingToken || resultStatus !== "checkout_open") return;\n    let stopped = false;\n    let attempts = 0;\n    const poll = async () => {\n      while (!stopped && attempts < 8) {\n        await new Promise((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, 1500));\n        if (stopped) return;\n        attempts += 1;\n        try {\n          const next = await resolvedApi.status(checkoutSessionId, bookingToken);\n          if (!stopped) setResult(next);\n          if (next.status !== "checkout_open" && next.status !== "draft") return;\n        } catch { /* the bounded confirmation fallback remains truthful */ }\n      }\n    };\n    void poll();\n    return () => { stopped = true; };\n  }, [api, bookingToken, checkoutSessionId, resultStatus]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!result || result.status === "checkout_open" || result.status === "draft") return;\n    sessionStorage.removeItem(pendingKey(event));\n    sessionStorage.removeItem(idempotencyKeyFor(event));\n  }, [result]);\n\n  const slotsByDay = useMemo(() => groupSlotsByDay(slots), [slots]);\n  const daySlots = pickedDay ? slotsByDay.get(dayKey(pickedDay)) ?? [] : [];\n  const cells = useMemo(() => buildMonthCells(monthAnchor), [monthAnchor]);\n  const weekDays = useMemo(() => slotDays(slots, 6), [slots]);\n\n  const choose = (slot: SingleSessionSlot) => {\n    setSelected(slot);\n    setError(null);\n    window.setTimeout(() => document.getElementById("single-session-name")?.focus(), 0);\n  };\n\n  // Named formEvent, not event: `event` is the booking event this page is for,\n  // and shadowing it here would silently key the session storage on a DOM object.\n  const submit = (formEvent: FormEvent) => {\n    formEvent.preventDefault();\n    if (!selected || submitPromise.current) return;\n    const work = (async () => {\n      setSubmitting(true);\n      setError(null);\n      try {\n        const normalizedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();\n        const normalizedGuests = [...new Set(guests.map((guest) => guest.trim().toLowerCase()).filter((guest) => guest && guest !== normalizedEmail))].slice(0, 10);\n        let idempotencyKey = sessionStorage.getItem(idempotencyKeyFor(event));\n        if (!idempotencyKey) {\n          idempotencyKey = crypto.randomUUID();\n          sessionStorage.setItem(idempotencyKeyFor(event), idempotencyKey);\n        }\n        const token = await resolvedApi.getTurnstileToken();\n        const hold = await resolvedApi.hold({\n          start_at: selected.start_at,\n          name: name.trim(), email: normalizedEmail, notes: notes.trim(), guests: normalizedGuests,\n          timezone, client_idempotency_key: idempotencyKey, turnstile_token: token,\n          ...(readPending(event)?.booking_token ? { booking_token: readPending(event)!.booking_token } : {}),\n        });\n        // A free event is already booked by the time hold returns: there is no\n        // checkout to send anyone to, so show the confirmation instead of\n        // navigating away to a Stripe page that does not exist.\n        if (!hold.checkout_url) {\n          sessionStorage.removeItem(pendingKey(event));\n          sessionStorage.removeItem(idempotencyKeyFor(event));\n          setResult(hold.booking ?? null);\n          return;\n        }\n        sessionStorage.setItem(pendingKey(event), JSON.stringify(hold));\n        resolvedApi.redirectToCheckout(hold.checkout_url);\n      } catch (e) {\n        const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : "booking_failed";\n        if (message.includes("security_check") || message === "verification_failed") {\n          setError(readableError(message));\n        } else if (message.includes("slot") || message.includes("unavailable") || message.includes("conflict")) {\n          const rejectedStartAt = selected.start_at;\n          sessionStorage.removeItem(idempotencyKeyFor(event));\n          setError("That time was just taken. We refreshed the available times so you can choose another.");\n          setSelected(null);\n          await loadAvailability(rejectedStartAt);\n          window.setTimeout(() => availabilityHeading.current?.focus(), 0);\n        } else setError(info?.requires_payment === false\n          ? "We couldn\'t confirm that time. Please try again."\n          : "We couldn\'t open checkout. Your card was not charged. Please try again.");\n      } finally {\n        setSubmitting(false);\n        submitPromise.current = null;\n      }\n    })();\n    submitPromise.current = work;\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <main className="booking-holo flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center" role="status" aria-live="polite"><Loader2 className="mr-2 h-5 w-5 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" />Loading available times</main>;\n  if (result) return <ResultView state={result} checkoutSessionId={checkoutSessionId} />;\n  if (resume) return <main className="booking-holo min-h-screen px-4 py-12 sm:py-20"><section className="booking-shadow mx-auto max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center sm:p-10">\n    <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Finish your payment</h1><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Your time is still held. Return to Stripe to finish booking it.</p>\n    <Button className="mt-6" onClick={() => resolvedApi.redirectToCheckout(resume.checkout_url)}>Return to secure checkout <ExternalLink className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" /></Button>\n    <Button variant="ghost" className="mt-3 block w-full" onClick={async () => { await resolvedApi.abandon(resume.checkout_session_id, resume.booking_token); sessionStorage.removeItem(pendingKey(event)); sessionStorage.removeItem(idempotencyKeyFor(event)); setResume(null); await loadAvailability(); }}>Release this time</Button>\n  </section></main>;\n  if (cancelToken && cancelPreview) return <main className="booking-holo min-h-screen px-4 py-12 sm:py-20"><section className="booking-shadow mx-auto max-w-xl rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card p-6 text-center sm:p-10">\n    <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Cancel this session?</h1><p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{formatSlot(cancelPreview.start_at)}</p><p className="mt-4 text-sm">Cancelling does not automatically issue a refund.</p>\n    <Button variant="destructive" className="mt-6" onClick={async () => { setSubmitting(true); try { setResult(await resolvedApi.cancel(cancelToken, true)); } finally { setSubmitting(false); } }} disabled={submitting}>{submitting ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" /> : null}Cancel session</Button>\n  </section></main>;\n\n  // A free event says "Free" rather than "$0.00", which reads like a bug.\n  const price = info\n    ? (info.requires_payment === false || info.amount_cents === 0\n        ? "Free"\n        : new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { style: "currency", currency: info.currency.toUpperCase() }).format(info.amount_cents / 100))\n    // Never a number while the event is unknown: a one-off link carries its own\n    // price, and guessing $45 at it would be a lie in the reader\'s favour.\n    : "—";\n  const title = info?.host.title ?? "LSAT Session";\n  // A one-off link the edge function has already marked spent. Nothing on this\n  // page is actionable in that state, so it replaces the picker outright.\n  const linkUsed = info?.available === false;\n  return <main className="booking-holo single-session-page min-h-screen px-3 py-4 sm:px-6 sm:py-8 lg:flex lg:items-center">\n    <section className="booking-shadow mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">\n      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)]">\n        <aside className="border-b border-border p-5 lg:border-b-0 lg:border-r lg:p-7">\n          <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary text-lg font-semibold text-primary-foreground" aria-hidden="true">J</div>\n          <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground">{info?.host.name ?? "Joshua"}</p>\n          <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">{title}</h1>\n          <dl className="mt-5 space-y-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Clock className="h-4 w-4" /><dt className="sr-only">Duration</dt><dd>{info ? durationLabel(info.duration_minutes) : "—"}</dd></div>\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Video className="h-4 w-4" /><dt className="sr-only">Location</dt><dd>Google Meet</dd></div>\n            <div className="flex items-center gap-2"><ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" /><dt className="sr-only">Price</dt><dd>{price}</dd></div>\n          </dl>\n          <div className="mt-6 border-t border-border pt-5"><Label htmlFor="booking-timezone" className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">Timezone</Label><select id="booking-timezone" value={timezone} onChange={(event) => setTimezone(event.target.value)} className="mt-2 min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 text-sm">{timezoneOptions.map((zone) => <option key={zone} value={zone}>{zone.replace(/_/g, " ")}</option>)}</select></div>\n        </aside>\n\n        <div className="min-w-0 p-4 sm:p-6 lg:p-7">\n          {error ? <div role="alert" className="mb-5 rounded-lg border border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-destructive">{readableError(error)}</div> : null}\n          {linkUsed ? <div className="py-16 text-center">\n            <CalendarDays className="mx-auto mb-3 h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground" />\n            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">This link has already been used</h2>\n            <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm text-muted-foreground">It booked one session and is now closed. If you still need a time, reply to Joshua and he\'ll send you a new link.</p>\n          </div> : selected ? <form onSubmit={submit} className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">\n            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="mb-4 -ml-3" onClick={() => setSelected(null)}><ChevronLeft className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />Back to times</Button>\n            <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold">Your details</h2>\n            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm"><span className="rounded-full bg-primary/10 px-3 py-1.5 font-medium text-primary">{formatSlot(selected.start_at)}</span><span className="rounded-full bg-muted px-3 py-1.5">{info ? `${info.duration_minutes} minutes` : ""}</span></div>\n            <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5">\n              <div><Label htmlFor="single-session-name">Your name*</Label><Input id="single-session-name" value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} required autoComplete="name" className="mt-2 min-h-11" /></div>\n              <div><Label htmlFor="single-session-email">Email address*</Label><Input id="single-session-email" type="email" value={email} onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)} required autoComplete="email" className="mt-2 min-h-11" /></div>\n              <div><Label htmlFor="single-session-notes">Additional notes</Label><Textarea id="single-session-notes" value={notes} maxLength={2000} onChange={(event) => setNotes(event.target.value)} placeholder="Please share anything that will help prepare for our meeting." className="mt-2 min-h-28" /></div>\n              <fieldset><legend className="text-sm font-medium">Guests</legend>{guests.map((guest, index) => <div key={index} className="mt-2 flex gap-2"><Input aria-label={`Guest ${index + 1} email address`} type="email" value={guest} onChange={(event) => setGuests((current) => current.map((value, guestIndex) => guestIndex === index ? event.target.value : value))} className="min-h-11" /><Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={`Remove guest ${index + 1}`} onClick={() => setGuests((current) => current.filter((_, guestIndex) => guestIndex !== index))}><Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" /></Button></div>)}\n                {guests.length < 10 ? <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="mt-2 -ml-3" onClick={() => setGuests((current) => [...current, ""])}><Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />Add guests</Button> : null}</fieldset>\n            </div>\n            <Button type="submit" className="mt-7 min-h-12 w-full" disabled={submitting}>{submitting ? "Confirm booking" : "Pay to book"}</Button>\n            <p className="mt-3 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">You\'ll finish payment securely on Stripe.</p>\n          </form> : <>\n            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><h2 ref={availabilityHeading} tabIndex={-1} className="text-xl font-semibold outline-none">Select a time</h2><div className="inline-flex rounded-lg border border-border p-1" aria-label="Time format"><button type="button" aria-pressed={hour12} onClick={() => setHour12(true)} className={cn("min-h-9 rounded-md px-3 text-xs font-medium", hour12 && "bg-primary text-primary-foreground")}>12h</button><button type="button" aria-pressed={!hour12} onClick={() => setHour12(false)} className={cn("min-h-9 rounded-md px-3 text-xs font-medium", !hour12 && "bg-primary text-primary-foreground")}>24h</button></div></div>\n            <div className="mt-5 grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[300px_minmax(0,1fr)]">\n              <div><div className="flex items-center justify-between"><Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Previous month" onClick={() => setMonthAnchor((date) => new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() - 1, 1))}><ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" /></Button><span className="text-sm font-semibold" aria-live="polite">{monthAnchor.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "long", year: "numeric" })}</span><Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Next month" onClick={() => setMonthAnchor((date) => new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, 1))}><ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" /></Button></div>\n                <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 text-center">{["S", "M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S"].map((day, index) => <span key={`${day}-${index}`} aria-hidden="true" className="py-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{day}</span>)}{cells.map((date, index) => date ? <button key={dayKey(date)} type="button" disabled={!slotsByDay.has(dayKey(date))} aria-label={date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric", year: "numeric" })} aria-pressed={pickedDay ? dayKey(date) === dayKey(pickedDay) : false} onClick={() => setPickedDay(date)} className={cn("mx-auto flex aspect-square w-full max-w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-sm focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring", slotsByDay.has(dayKey(date)) ? "bg-primary/10 font-medium hover:bg-primary/20" : "text-muted-foreground/35", pickedDay && dayKey(date) === dayKey(pickedDay) && "bg-primary text-primary-foreground hover:bg-primary")}>{date.getDate()}</button> : <span key={`empty-${index}`} />)}</div>\n              </div>\n              <div data-testid="single-session-mobile-slots" className="xl:hidden"><h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">{pickedDay ? pickedDay.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "long", month: "long", day: "numeric" }) : "Choose a day"}</h3><div className="mt-3 grid gap-2">{daySlots.map((slot) => <button key={slot.start_at} type="button" onClick={() => choose(slot)} aria-label={formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)} className="min-h-12 w-full rounded-lg border border-primary/40 px-4 text-sm font-medium text-primary hover:bg-primary/5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring">{formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)}</button>)}</div></div>\n              <div data-testid="single-session-desktop-week" className="hidden min-w-0 xl:block"><div className="grid grid-cols-6 gap-2">{weekDays.map((date) => { const available = slotsByDay.get(dayKey(date)) ?? []; return <div key={dayKey(date)} className="min-w-0"><div className="mb-2 text-center"><div className="text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground">{date.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "short" })}</div><div className="text-sm font-semibold">{date.getDate()}</div></div><div className="space-y-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/40 p-2 [background-image:repeating-linear-gradient(135deg,hsl(var(--muted))_0,hsl(var(--muted))_1px,transparent_1px,transparent_7px)]">{available.length ? available.map((slot) => <button key={slot.start_at} type="button" onClick={() => choose(slot)} aria-label={formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)} className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-primary/40 bg-card px-1 text-xs font-medium text-primary hover:bg-primary/5 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring">{formatTime(slot.start_at, hour12, timezone)}</button>) : <div className="h-11" aria-label="Unavailable" />}</div></div>; })}</div></div>\n            </div>\n            {!slots.length ? <div className="mt-8 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground"><CalendarDays className="mx-auto mb-2 h-5 w-5" />No open times in the current booking window.</div> : null}\n          </>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  </main>;\n}\n\r\n'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '  </>\r\n);\r\n\r\n// ── Nav ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\r\nexport const AcaNav = ({ onSignIn }: { onSignIn: () => void }) => {\r\n  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = React.useState(false);\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n    const on = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 24);\r\n    on();\r\n    window.addEventListener(\'scroll\', on, { passive: true });\r\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\'scroll\', on);\r\n  }, []);\r\n  return (\r\n    <nav className={`aca-nav${scrolled ? \' is-scrolled\' : \'\'}`}>\r\n      <div className="aca-nav-inner">\r\n        <Link to="/" className="aca-wordmark" aria-label="LSAT U home">\r\n          LSAT&thinsp;<em>U</em>\r\n        </Link>\r\n        <button type="button" className="aca-nav-signin" onClick={onSignIn}>\r\n          Sign in\r\n        </button>\r\n      </div>\r\n    </nav>\r\n  );\r\n};\r\n\r\n// Play while on screen, pause off screen. play() can reject mid-scroll (the\r\n// pause() race aborts it), which would otherwise strand the film paused in\r\n// full view; retry shortly after while it should still be playing.\r\nfunction usePlayWhileVisible(videoRef: React.RefObject<HTMLVideoElement>, enabled: boolean) {\r\n  React.useEffect(() => {\r\n/* Booking product theme: light Calendly-style base with a deep burgundy\n   holographic accent. Scoped under .booking-holo so it can wrap both the\n   admin workspace and the student-facing booking pages without touching\n   the rest of the app. Overrides the shadcn HSL tokens locally. */\n\n.booking-holo {\n  --background: 350 20% 99%;\n  --foreground: 345 30% 12%;\n  --card: 0 0% 100%;\n  --card-foreground: 345 30% 12%;\n  --popover: 0 0% 100%;\n  --popover-foreground: 345 30% 12%;\n  --primary: 347 70% 27%;\n  --primary-foreground: 0 0% 100%;\n  --secondary: 348 30% 96%;\n  --secondary-foreground: 347 55% 25%;\n  --muted: 347 20% 96%;\n  --muted-foreground: 345 12% 44%;\n  --accent: 348 45% 94%;\n  --accent-foreground: 347 60% 24%;\n  --destructive: 0 72% 45%;\n  --destructive-foreground: 0 0% 100%;\n  --border: 345 18% 90%;\n  --input: 345 18% 87%;\n  --ring: 347 70% 35%;\n  color: hsl(var(--foreground));\n  background-color: hsl(var(--background));\n}\n\n.holo-text {\n  color: hsl(var(--primary));\n}\n\n.holo-surface {\n  /* Static rich burgundy gradient — deliberately NOT animated. The shadcn\n     Button base applies `transition-all` and swaps background-color on hover,\n     which fights any background keyframe animation frame-by-frame and reads\n     as flicker. Buttons stay still; only the wordmark carries the sheen. */\n  background: linear-gradient(120deg, #4f0d1c, #6e1423 45%, #8e1d3a) !important;\n  color: #fff !important;\n}\n\n.holo-surface:hover { filter: brightness(1.12); }\n\n.holo-ring { box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px hsl(347 70% 35% / 0.35), 0 8px 30px hsl(347 70% 25% / 0.12); }\n\n/* Soft burgundy-tinted elevation, the Calendly modal-shadow trick re-tinted. */\n.booking-shadow { box-shadow: 0 24px 72px hsl(345 60% 20% / 0.09), 0 0 20px hsl(345 60% 20% / 0.06); }\n\n@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {\n  .holo-surface { animation: none; }\n  .single-session-page *,\n  .single-session-page *::before,\n  .single-session-page *::after {\n    scroll-behavior: auto !important;\n    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;\n    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;\n    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;\n  }\n}\n\n/* The booking theme is deliberately light, which is right on the public\n   booking page a prospect lands on cold. Inside the dark classroom shell it\n   rendered as a 688x647 pure-white slab in a black page — the most jarring\n   thing a student meets on their first click into dark mode.\n\n   This re-voices the same burgundy palette dark, and is scoped to the\n   classroom so /book/* stays light for prospects. It cannot live in the app\'s\n   `.dark` class: that class is a deliberate alias that mirrors `.light`\n   (see index.css), so every shadcn token in the app stays light by design and\n   the classroom draws its own dark theme separately. */\nhtml.dark .classroom-root .booking-holo {\n  color-scheme: dark;\n  --background: 345 12% 9%;\n  --foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --card: 345 12% 13%;\n  --card-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --popover: 345 12% 13%;\n  --popover-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  /* Lightness is set by contrast, not by matching the light theme\'s 27%: the\n     accent is used as text on the card, and 58% measured 4.13:1 against it. */\n  --primary: 347 68% 68%;\n  --primary-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --secondary: 345 12% 19%;\n  --secondary-foreground: 345 15% 93%;\n  --muted: 345 10% 18%;\n  --muted-foreground: 345 10% 70%;\n  --accent: 345 15% 23%;\n  --accent-foreground: 345 15% 94%;\n  --destructive: 0 65% 58%;\n  --destructive-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --border: 345 10% 25%;\n  --input: 345 10% 27%;\n  --ring: 347 62% 52%;\n}\n\n/* DialogContent is portaled outside `.classroom-root`, so carry the same\n   classroom-dark tokens on the booking dialog itself. Public booking remains\n   light because only the dialog opts into this selector. */\nhtml.dark .booking-dialog.booking-holo {\n  color-scheme: dark;\n  --background: 345 12% 9%;\n  --foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --card: 345 12% 13%;\n  --card-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --popover: 345 12% 13%;\n  --popover-foreground: 345 15% 95%;\n  --primary: 347 68% 68%;\n  --primary-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --secondary: 345 12% 19%;\n  --secondary-foreground: 345 15% 93%;\n  --muted: 345 10% 18%;\n  --muted-foreground: 345 10% 70%;\n  --accent: 345 15% 23%;\n  --accent-foreground: 345 15% 94%;\n  --destructive: 0 65% 58%;\n  --destructive-foreground: 345 30% 10%;\n  --border: 345 10% 25%;\n  --input: 345 10% 27%;\n  --ring: 347 62% 52%;\n}\n\r\n/* Main Conclusion bootcamp scoped styles (active only inside .mc-bootcamp). */\n@import "./bootcamps/main-conclusion/styles/scoped.css";\n\n@tailwind base;\n@tailwind components;\n@tailwind utilities;\n\n/* Definition of the design system. All colors, gradients, fonts, etc should be defined here.\nAll colors MUST be HSL.\n*/\n\n@layer base {\n  :root {\n    color-scheme: dark;\n\n    /* LSAT U — Dark-first design system.\n       Tokens resolve to the dark palette by default.\n       Add class="light" to <html> to switch to light mode.\n    */\n    --background: 0 0% 3.9%;         /* neutral-950 */\n    --foreground: 0 0% 98%;           /* near white */\n\n    --card: 0 0% 7%;                  /* neutral-900 equivalent */\n    --card-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    --popover: 0 0% 7%;\n    --popover-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    --primary: 0 0% 98%;              /* white — primary interactive on dark */\n    --primary-foreground: 0 0% 3.9%; /* dark text behind white elements */\n\n    --secondary: 0 0% 14.9%;          /* neutral-800 */\n    --secondary-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    --muted: 0 0% 14.9%;\n    --muted-foreground: 0 0% 63.9%;   /* neutral-500 */\n\n    --accent: 0 0% 14.9%;\n    --accent-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    /* Status colors */\n    --success: 142 71% 45%;\n    --success-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    --warning: 38 92% 50%;\n    --warning-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    --destructive: 0 72% 51%;\n    --destructive-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    /* Attempt-state semantic tokens.\n       Used by drill / flashcard / playground feedback across topic bootcamps.\n       Hue-distinct from --success / --destructive so attempt feedback never\n       collides visually with system status. */\n    --state-correct: 160 84% 39%;     /* emerald-500 */\n    --state-correct-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    --state-incorrect: 350 89% 60%;   /* rose-500 */\n    --state-incorrect-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    /* Spaced-repetition / self-rate tokens.\n       Same hues as state-correct / state-incorrect at the source, but kept\n       under distinct names so future hue shifts to wrong/right cannot\n       silently reframe a self-report as a judgment. */\n    --state-again: 350 89% 60%;       /* rose-500, aliased on purpose */\n    --state-again-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    --state-easy: 160 84% 39%;        /* emerald-500, aliased on purpose */\n    --state-easy-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    /* Surface-warn semantic — bonus / stretch / non-blocking notice. */\n    --state-warn: 38 92% 50%;         /* amber-500, mirrors --warning */\n    --state-warn-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n\n    --border: 0 0% 14.9%;             /* neutral-800 */\n    --input: 0 0% 14.9%;\n    --ring: 0 0% 83.1%;               /* neutral-300 */\n\n    --radius: 0.5rem;\n\n    /* Shadows */\n    --shadow-sm: 0 1px 3px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);\n    --shadow: 0 2px 12px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);\n    --shadow-md: 0 4px 20px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);\n    --shadow-lg: 0 8px 32px 0 rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7);\n    --shadow-glow: 0 0 24px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06);\n\n    /* Surface elevation */\n    --surface-elevated: 0 0% 10%;\n\n    /* Text hierarchy */\n    --text-primary: 0 0% 98%;\n    --text-secondary: 0 0% 63.9%;\n    --text-tertiary: 0 0% 45%;\n\n    --sidebar-background: 0 0% 3.9%;\n    --sidebar-foreground: 0 0% 63.9%;\n    --sidebar-primary: 0 0% 98%;\n    --sidebar-primary-foreground: 0 0% 3.9%;\n    --sidebar-accent: 0 0% 14.9%;\n    --sidebar-accent-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    --sidebar-border: 0 0% 14.9%;\n    --sidebar-ring: 0 0% 83.1%;\n\n    /* ── Teacher workspace accent ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n     * The Desk\'s warm amber, promoted from the hardcoded #c8841f it was written\n     * as. This is THE accent for every primary action in the teacher workspace;\n     * no second accent colour is allowed there. Hue-locked ramp so the steps\n     * read as one colour at different weights. Theme-independent on purpose:\n     * the workspace is always the light gray format, never the dark shell.\n     */\n    --teacher-accent: 36 73% 45%;           /* #c8841f — fills, icons, rules */\n    --teacher-accent-hover: 36 76% 39%;     /* #b07418 — hover on filled */\n    --teacher-accent-strong: 36 79% 30%;    /* #8a5a10 — text on tinted fills */\n    --teacher-accent-soft: 37 71% 58%;      /* #e0a64a — light step */\n    --teacher-accent-foreground: 0 0% 100%; /* text on a filled accent */\n  }\n\n  /* ── Light mode ── add class="light" to <html> to activate ───────────────── */\n  .light {\n    color-scheme: light;\n    --background: 0 0% 99%;\n    --foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --card: 0 0% 100%;\n    --card-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --popover: 0 0% 100%;\n    --popover-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --primary: 0 0% 7%;\n    --primary-foreground: 0 0% 99%;\n    --secondary: 0 0% 95%;\n    --secondary-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --muted: 0 0% 95%;\n    --muted-foreground: 0 0% 45%;\n    --accent: 0 0% 92%;\n    --accent-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --destructive: 0 72% 51%;\n    --destructive-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    --border: 0 0% 88%;\n    --input: 0 0% 90%;\n    --ring: 0 0% 20%;\n    --surface-elevated: 0 0% 97%;\n    --text-primary: 0 0% 7%;\n    --text-secondary: 0 0% 45%;\n    --text-tertiary: 0 0% 55%;\n    --sidebar-background: 0 0% 98%;\n    --sidebar-foreground: 0 0% 28%;\n    --sidebar-primary: 0 0% 7%;\n    --sidebar-primary-foreground: 0 0% 99%;\n    --sidebar-accent: 0 0% 92%;\n    --sidebar-accent-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --sidebar-border: 0 0% 88%;\n    --sidebar-ring: 0 0% 20%;\n  }\n\n  /* .dark mirrors .light — reserved alias */\n  .dark {\n    color-scheme: light;\n    --background: 0 0% 99%;\n    --foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --card: 0 0% 100%;\n    --card-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --popover: 0 0% 100%;\n    --popover-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --primary: 0 0% 7%;\n    --primary-foreground: 0 0% 99%;\n    --secondary: 0 0% 95%;\n    --secondary-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --muted: 0 0% 95%;\n    --muted-foreground: 0 0% 45%;\n    --accent: 0 0% 92%;\n    --accent-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --destructive: 0 72% 51%;\n    --destructive-foreground: 0 0% 98%;\n    --border: 0 0% 88%;\n    --input: 0 0% 90%;\n    --ring: 0 0% 20%;\n    --sidebar-background: 0 0% 98%;\n    --sidebar-foreground: 0 0% 28%;\n    --sidebar-primary: 0 0% 7%;\n    --sidebar-primary-foreground: 0 0% 99%;\n    --sidebar-accent: 0 0% 92%;\n    --sidebar-accent-foreground: 0 0% 7%;\n    --sidebar-border: 0 0% 88%;\n    --sidebar-ring: 0 0% 20%;\n  }\n}\n\n@layer base {\n  * {\n    @apply border-border;\n  }\n\n  /* Focus indicator floor.\n     Zero specificity via :where(), so any component that defines its own focus\n     treatment still wins (the Academy\'s cyan, the scoped bootcamp rings). This\n     exists so a control that defines nothing is never left without one: the\n     shared Button shipped for five months with no visible focus at all, because\n     it removed the browser default and replaced it with a ring class that did\n     not exist pointing at a token that was never defined. */\n  :where(a[href], button, input, select, textarea, summary, [tabindex]:not([tabindex=\'-1\'])):focus-visible {\n    outline: 2px solid hsl(var(--foreground));\n    outline-offset: 2px;\n  }\n\n  body {\n    @apply bg-background text-foreground;\n    font-family: \'Lexend\', \'Outfit\', ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;\n    font-size: 14px;\n    line-height: 1.6;\n    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;\n    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;\n    font-feature-settings: "rlig" 1, "calt" 1, "cv11" 1;\n  }\n\n  /* Font lock for LSAT-question / Smart Drill surfaces: these must keep the\n     original Outfit stack even though the rest of the app moved to Lexend. */\n  .font-lsat-question {\n    font-family: \'Outfit\', ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;\n  }\n\n  @media (min-width: 640px) {\n    body {\n      font-size: 16px;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Touch-friendly spacing and targets */\n  @media (max-width: 767px) {\n    input, textarea, select, button {\n      font-size: 16px; /* Prevents zoom on iOS */\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Section stagger entry animation */\n  @keyframes fade-in-up {\n    from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); }\n    to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }\n  }\n  .animate-section {\n    animation: fade-in-up 0.45s cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1) both;\n  }\n\n  /* Highlight styles - zero layout shift */\n  .hl {\n    display: inline;\n    padding: 0;\n    margin: 0;\n    border: 0;\n    line-height: inherit;\n    letter-spacing: inherit;\n    word-spacing: inherit;\n    vertical-align: baseline;\n    white-space: pre-wrap;\n    background: none;\n    mix-blend-mode: normal;\n    border-radius: 0;\n    -webkit-box-decoration-break: clone;\n    box-decoration-break: clone;\n  }\n}\n\n/* 3D flip utilities (used by bootcamp Flashcard) */\n.perspective-1200 { perspective: 1200px; }\n.preserve-3d { transform-style: preserve-3d; }\n.backface-hidden { -webkit-backface-visibility: hidden; backface-visibility: hidden; }\n.rotate-y-180 { transform: rotateY(180deg); }\n\r\n'}]

public.single_session_bookings (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  start_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  end_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  name text,\r\n  email text,\r\n  notes text,\r\n  guests jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,\r\n  client_idempotency_key text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  draft_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  checkout_request_fingerprint text,\r\n  checkout_fingerprint_disposition text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'none',\r\n  stripe_checkout_session_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_checkout_url text,\r\n  stripe_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_payment_intent_id text,\r\n  amount_cents integer,\r\n  currency text,\r\n  livemode boolean,\r\n  user_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\r\n  booking_token_hash text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\r\n  booking_token_expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\r\n  cancel_token_hash text UNIQUE,\r\n  cancel_token_expires_at timestamptz,\r\n  stripe_refund_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  stripe_refund_status text,\r\n  gcal_event_id text UNIQUE,\r\n  meet_url text,\r\n  lifecycle_version integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',\r\n  status_changed_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  terminal_reason text,\r\n  ip_hash text,\r\n  sweep_worker_id text,\r\n  sweep_lease_token uuid,\r\n  sweep_lease_until timestamptz,\r\n  sweep_attempts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\r\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_duration_chk CHECK (end_at = start_at + interval '70 minutes'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_guests_chk CHECK (jsonb_typeof(guests) = 'array' AND jsonb_array_length(guests) <= 10),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_lifecycle_version_chk CHECK (lifecycle_version >= 0),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_amount_chk CHECK (amount_cents IS NULL OR amount_cents = 4500),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_currency_chk CHECK (currency IS NULL OR lower(currency) = 'usd'),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_status_chk CHECK (status IN (\r\n    'draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked', 'cancelled', 'expired', 'abandoned',\r\n    'refund_pending', 'refunded', 'refund_failed'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_fingerprint_disposition_chk CHECK (checkout_fingerprint_disposition IN (\r\n    'none', 'persisted', 'attached', 'stripe_expired', 'stripe_absent'\r\n  )),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_checkout_expiry_chk CHECK (\r\n    (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (stripe_checkout_session_id IS NOT NULL AND stripe_expires_at IS NOT NULL)\r\n  ),\r\n  CONSTRAINT single_session_cancel_token_chk CHECK (\r\n    (cancel_token_hash IS NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at IS NULL)\r\n    OR (cancel_token_hash IS NOT NULL AND cancel_token_expires_at = start_at)\r\n  )\r\n);\r\n\r\nALTER TABLE public.single_session_bookings\r\n  ADD CONSTRAINT single_session_no_occupying_overlap\r\n  EXCLUDE USING gist (tstzrange(start_at, end_at, '[)') WITH &&)\r\n  WHERE (status IN ('draft', 'checkout_open', 'booked'));\r\n\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_start_at_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (start_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_status_expiry_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (status, stripe_expires_at, draft_expires_at);\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_email_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (lower(email)) WHERE email IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_user_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (user_id, start_at) WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;\r\nCREATE INDEX single_session_bookings_sweep_idx\r\n  ON public.single_session_bookings (sweep_lease_until, status, [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'import { useMemo } from "react";\nimport { useParams } from "react-router-dom";\nimport CalendlyBookingCard, { type BookingApi } from "@/components/booking/CalendlyBookingCard";\n\n// Public, no-login booking page for per-student links (/book/:slug).\n// Talks to the anonymous `public-booking` edge function using the publishable\n// (anon) key ONLY — never a user session. The function ignores Authorization\n// and resolves identity from the slug, so even if a logged-in user opens this\n// page, their JWT is never used (Codex R1 #1 defense).\n//\n// This page intentionally has NO app chrome (sidebar/nav) and shows no student\n// PII — the host is always "Joshua".\n\nconst SUPABASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL as string;\nconst ANON_KEY = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY as string;\n\nasync function callPublic(slug: string, action: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {\n  const res = await fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/public-booking`, {\n    method: "POST",\n    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", apikey: [REDACTED], Authorization: [REDACTED] ${ANON_KEY}` },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ slug, action, ...extra }),\n  });\n  const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));\n  if (!res.ok || data?.error) {\n    throw new Error(data?.error || `request_failed_${res.status}`);\n  }\n  return data;\n}\n\nexport default function PublicBooking() {\n  const { slug = "" } = useParams();\n\n  const api: BookingApi = useMemo(() => ({\n    loadInfo: async () => {\n      const d = await callPublic(slug, "info");\n      return {\n        pass: d.pass ?? null,\n        bookings: (d.redemptions ?? []).map((row: { id: string; start_at: string; status: string; meet_url?: string | null }) => ({ source: "redemption" as const, ...row })),\n      };\n    },\n    loadSlots: async () => {\n      const d = await callPublic(slug, "slots");\n      return { pass: d.pass ?? null, slots: d.slots ?? [] };\n    },\n    book: async (startAtIso, idempotencyKey, studentNote) => {\n      await callPublic(slug, "book", {\n        start_at: startAtIso,\n        idempotency_key: idempotencyKey,\n        ...(studentNote ? { student_note: studentNote } : {}),\n      });\n    },\n    uploadFiles: async (files, idempotencyKey) => {\n      const fd = new FormData();\n      fd.append("slug", slug);\n      fd.append("idempotency_key", idempotencyKey);\n      for (const entry of files) {\n        fd.append("files", entry.file, entry.file.name);\n        fd.append("client_upload_ids", entry.clientUploadId);\n      }\n      const res = await fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/booking-upload`, {\n        method: "POST",\n        headers: { apikey: [REDACTED], Authorization: [REDACTED] ${ANON_KEY}` },\n        body: fd,\n      });\n      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      if (!res.ok || data?.error) throw new Error(data?.error || `upload_failed_${res.status}`);\n      return {\n        uploaded: data.uploaded ?? files.length,\n        clientUploadIds: (data.files ?? []).map((entry: { client_upload_id: string }) => entry.client_upload_id),\n      };\n    },\n    discardUpload: async (clientUploadId, idempotencyKey) => {\n      const res = await fetch(`${SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/booking-upload`, {\n        method: "POST",\n        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", apikey: [REDACTED], Authorization: [REDACTED] ${ANON_KEY}` },\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          action: "discard", slug, idempotency_key: idempotencyKey, client_upload_id: clientUploadId,\n        }),\n      });\n      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      if (!res.ok || data?.error) throw new Error(data?.error || `discard_failed_${res.status}`);\n    },\n  }), [slug]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className="booking-holo min-h-screen px-4 py-10">\n      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-4xl">\n        <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5">\n          <div className="holo-surface flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-sm font-bold">L</div>\n          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-foreground">Booking</h1>\n        </div>\n        <CalendlyBookingCard api={api} />\n        <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">Powered by LSAT U</p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\r\nsrc/components/booking\\BookingConfirmDialog.test.tsx:3:import BookingConfirmDialog from "./BookingConfirmDialog";\nsrc/components/booking\\BookingConfirmDialog.test.tsx:5:describe("BookingConfirmDialog", () => {\nsrc/components/booking\\BookingConfirmDialog.test.tsx:11:      <BookingConfirmDialog\nsrc/components/booking\\BookingConfirmDialog.tsx:93:export default function BookingConfirmDialog({ open, onOpenChange, slotIso, durationMinutes, book, onBooked, onCommitError, upload, theme = "booking" }: Props) {\nsrc/components/booking\\CalendlyBookingCard.tsx:3:import BookingConfirmDialog from "@/components/booking/BookingConfirmDialog";\nsrc/components/booking\\CalendlyBookingCard.tsx:226:              <span>Google Meet link provided on confirmation.</span>\nsrc/components/booking\\CalendlyBookingCard.tsx:348:      <BookingConfirmDialog\n'}]
